#132763 3 2006-01-16T01:05:29Z Greengooguy49

#132771 4 2006-01-16T01:35:52Z Greengooguy49
If anyone else needs help like i did

Quote
Well, If they are on a website, you should...
1. Go to My Controls.
2. Go to Edit Signiture.
3. Put the website with the image in IMG tags. (One at a time.)
4. You should be done!

If your pictures are on a file, then you should...
1. Go to a place like Image Shack.
2. Host your image there.
3. Come back to the Control Panel witha link to the image, and put it in IMG tags.
4. You should be done!

So there! Hope this helps!

#250210 24 2006-11-05T03:07:14Z Greengooguy49
Quote: TornadoTK, November 05, 2006 03:02 am
"Well I could tell you that if it was released on PS2, PSP, XBOX, or PC I would be buying it immediatly! It sounds great. "

More realistically, this would launch on a next-gen console. You know, since it's not in development, and all.


"I was just wondering that when you put down your own check points in a race... How would know where to put them? Like would you be looking at a map to put them or do you do like a test drive and drive around till you find the fastest route and then put them in where you think you will need them?"

You put the checkpoints wherever you feel. In A-to-B races, your only goal is to make it to point B as fast as you can. If you have a Muscle car, I'm sure you'd want to head for the freeway, or any straightaways, since that's where you do your best. If your speed isn't too great, but that shorter, windy road is perfect for a car with better handling, you can take that road instead. Checkpoints are unnecessary markers that let you mark a path before a race starts, so you never get lost. A map will open up right before a race, and you can place down checkpoints as you please. You can place checkpoints in areas that you don't know about, but what good will that do you? If you fail a race, however, you can always prepare better for the second go. It's like mapping a route in real life, except for the fact that you can't have a huge arrow float over your car.


"I thought that if you were to put in check points for a race there should be a set number of check points that you could put in this would vary depending on how long the race was and how far though the game it was."

Well, some people want many points, and some would use very few, so there will just be a set limit. That way, you can use as many as you want. How you race is your decision.

Well it all sounds like you have things sussed out. When are you going to submit it? And before you submit it I would reccomend showing it to us before so tha we can proof read things.

#253312 5 2006-11-12T03:30:28Z Greengooguy49
So when replying to a topic or starting your own one. Can you see the button that says IMG above the area where you type your message. Well when you click on that button it comes up with a new dialogue window which says to post the URL for that image. To get a URL for your image you have to go to an image hosting site. eg: http://photobucket.com/ There they will let you upload your pictures to their servers nd they will give you a URl forthat image you then post that URL with the tags [IMG]*insert Url of image here*[/URL]. But by clicking the button I mentioned befor it automaticly makes the [IMG] tags for you.

Another way to post an image is to click the link below the area where you post your writing which says File Attachments and browse your computer till you find the image.



Hope that helped. If you have any other troubles then post again.

#255346 84 2006-11-16T03:47:22Z Greengooguy49
Human Rights Attitudes to the death penalty essay

The death penalty is used only with the worst crimes, mainly murder. Should or shouldnt it be used?

By having the death penalty in place it creates deterrence to the potential murders that may face the threat of the death penalty if caught. Ernest van den Haag who studied the fact of deterrence wroteCapital punishment is most likely to deter more then other forms of punishment because people fear death more than anything else and what ever people fear the most is more likely to deter most.

The rebuttal to deterrence is that by having the death penalty in place it makes the society more brutalised which increases the likelihood of murders. The death penalty isnt deterrence because for the some people. These people are the ones who have and potentially will commit murder, because of those people most of them have thought it through or either dont expect to be caught.

Retribution is another word for revenge. This can be used to describe the death penalty as in most cases the family that have lost someone in their family mainly want the killer to face the death penalty to make things fair. But a killer also shouldnt be able to live in a jail with three meals a day, clean sheets, TV, family visits while a family lives on mourning, the whole time with a loved one gone forever.

In having the death penalty there is a risk that they may accidentally accuse the wrong person only to execute an innocent victim. This was very common as found in a recent study by the Colombia University Law school. It found that two thirds of the capital punishment victims contained some serious errors. Some resulting in a innocent person executed.

But if the innocent person is executed, they have had their chance to prove themselves innocent, and any person who is proved innocent will have the death penalty removed.

There isnt any proof that the death penalty works better than life in prison. They achieve the same thing, stop the murder from striking again and keep the communities out of danger.

#256593 104 2006-11-18T19:43:16Z Greengooguy49
Quote: Black Sheep Boy, November 16, 2006 04:46 am
There are... many, many flaws in that. And a lot of incorrect grammar- not the sort of thing I'd like to submit as a proper essay, but anyway...

Mainly...
Quote: GreenGooGuy, 2006-11-16T03:47:22Z
The death penalty isnt deterrence because for the some people.

Meaning?

Anywho, nobody who commits crimes punishable by death is thinking straight. A life behind bars looks no more appealing than death. Either way, the only way in which somebody will commit a crime of the magnitude which can result in the death penalty is if they honestly believe they can get away with it, or they can't even consider the punishment rationally at the time they commit the crime.

Quote: GreenGooGuy, 2006-11-16T03:47:22Z
But if the innocent person is executed, they have had their chance to prove themselves innocent, and any person who is proved innocent will have the death penalty removed.

So you're... basically blaming any innocent person being put to death for allowing themselves to be put to death? And consider- the evidence which can arise to prove somebody innocent may only arrive after the person in question has been put to death. (Indeed, in a few odd occasions I've read about, just that has happened.) The death penalty is retractable, and you cannot blame the person being put to death for being put to death.

I'm fairly certain that they would be doing everything they could to avoid, you know, dying.

Quote: GreenGooGuy, 2006-11-16T03:47:22Z
But a killer also shouldnt be able to live in a jail with three meals a day, clean sheets, TV, family visits while a family lives on mourning, the whole time with a loved one gone forever.

People do not 'luxuriate' in prison. Prison is not a nice place to be. Having no freedom, having your life controlled and restricted in almost every aspect, is not a nice thing.

Prison is bad. Very few (though it isn't unheard of) people expressively try to get into prisons. There's a reason for this, being that prisons aren't very nice places. A cry for revenge beyond a prison sentance does not equate to a prison sentance being a light punishment.

Quote: GreenGooGuy, 2006-11-16T03:47:22Z
The rebuttal to deterrence is that by having the death penalty in place it makes the society more brutalised which increases the likelihood of murders.
I've never heard that one before... but I'd be catious if I were to try to blame the death penalty for people commiting crimes. Blame unfair distribution of wealth, unhealthy upbringings, hell- even violent video games and television programs- but I don't really see the death penalty making a great deal of difference here.

Though it has been shown, numerous times, that even populations and countries which are, by a comfortable margin, in favour of the death penalty will, after it has been removed, soon have the overwhelming majority against it. Death Penalty breeds people who like the death penlaty, and the lack of breeds the people who are against it.

However, again, I wouldn't try to tie this in with people acting violently. At all.

Basically, I think you've barely scraped the surface of the issue with that
extremely short essay.
Well what more do I have to really add?



Ok here is the revised version:

Human Rights Attitudes to the death penalty essay

The death penalty is used only with the worst crimes, mainly murder. Should or shouldnt it be used?

The death penalty deterrence to potential murderers that may face the threat of the death penalty if caught. Ernest van den Haag who studied the fact of deterrence wrote Capital punishment is most likely to deter more then other forms of punishment because people fear death more than anything else and what ever people fear the most is more likely to deter most.

The death penalty makes society more brutalised which increases the likelihood of murders. The people who have and potentially will commit murder either dont expect to be caught or most of them have weighed up the consequences of their actions.

Capital Punishment is a form of retribution. Retribution is another word for revenge. In most cases the family that have lost a family member mainly want the killer to face the death penalty to make things fair. But a killer also shouldnt be able to live in a jail with three meals a day, clean sheets, TV and family visits while a family lives on mourning, the whole time with a loved one gone forever.

In having the death penalty there is a risk that the wrong person may accidentally be accused, leading to the execution of an innocent victim. This was very common as found in a recent study by the Colombia University Law School. The study found that two thirds of capital punishment cases contained some serious errors, sometimes resulting in an innocent person to be executed.

There isnt any proof that the death penalty works better than life in prison. They achieve the same thing, stop the murderer from striking again and keep the communities out of danger.

Human Rights – Attitudes to the death penalty essay

These people are the ones who have and potentially will commit murder, because of those people most of them have thought it through or either don’t expect to be caught.

But a killer also shouldn’t be able to live in a jail with three meals a day, clean sheets, TV, family visits while a family lives on mourning, the whole time with a loved one gone forever.

#259636 17 2006-11-25T21:25:42Z Greengooguy49
The thing is that when you are saving it with GIMP you want to have the original copy with all of the layers on and stuff s you save it as a .xcf

But when you want to mae it into an image to use somewhere you have to save it as somehting else like a .jpg or a .png


That was the only thing that was wrong, you saved it as a .xcf and only GIMP recognizes .xcf's



Hope that helped you. And I would reccomen goign through this tutorial before you make anything else. It's really helpful: http://www.gimptalk.com/forum/topic/tutori...-Hour-20-1.html
